cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(cahn_hilliard_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(cahn_hilliard cahn_hilliard.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(cahn_hilliard PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(cahn_hilliard m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
Each timestep has two stencil passes, with a required synchronization between them. I’ll keep one OpenMP team active across timesteps, divide contiguous rows among threads, and vectorize the row interiors while preserving the clamped boundaries and arithmetic order.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Row pointers keep the stencil accesses contiguous and hoist Y/Z boundary
// calculations out of the SIMD loop. X boundaries are handled separately.
inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* ym, const double* yp,
                              const double* zm, const double* zp,
                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
    const double center = row[x];
    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * center) / dx2;
    const double cyy = (yp[x] + ym[x] - 2.0 * center) / dy2;
    const double czz = (zp[x] + zm[x] - 2.0 * center) / dz2;
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// All threads in the persistent team call these worksharing routines.
// The implicit barriers ensure each stencil reads a complete input field.
void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;
    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            if (nx == 0) continue;
            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
            const double* row = c + base;
            const double* ym = y > 0 ? row - nx : row;
            const double* yp = y + 1 < ny ? row + nx : row;
            const double* zm = z > 0 ? row - plane : row;
            const double* zp = z + 1 < nz ? row + plane : row;
            double* out = mu + base;
            const auto writeCell = [&](size_t x, size_t xn, size_t xp) {
                const double cv = row[x];
                out[x] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(row, ym, yp, zm, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2);
            };
            writeCell(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                writeCell(x, x - 1, x + 1);
            }
            if (nx > 1) {
                writeCell(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;
    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            if (nx == 0) continue;
            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
            const double* row = mu + base;
            const double* ym = y > 0 ? row - nx : row;
            const double* yp = y + 1 < ny ? row + nx : row;
            const double* zm = z > 0 ? row - plane : row;
            const double* zp = z + 1 < nz ? row + plane : row;
            const double* old = cold + base;
            double* out = cnew + base;
            const auto writeCell = [&](size_t x, size_t xn, size_t xp) {
                out[x] = old[x] + dt * D *
                         computeLaplacian(row, ym, yp, zm, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2);
            };
            writeCell(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                writeCell(x, x - 1, x + 1);
            }
            if (nx > 1) {
                writeCell(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < vol; ++idx) {
        // Generate the same deterministic pseudo-random value in [-1, 1].
        const double pseudo = ((((idx + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
        c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* ym, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zm, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * center) / dx2;
+    const double cyy = (yp[x] + ym[x] - 2.0 * center) / dy2;
+    const double czz = (zp[x] + zm[x] - 2.0 * center) / dz2;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }

fds = {}
def compile_source(name, source, extra=()):
    if name in fds:
        os.close(fds.pop(name))
    fd = os.memfd_create(name, 0)
    result = subprocess.run([\"g++\", \"-std=c++20\", \"-O3\", \"-march=native\", \"-Wall\", \"-Wextra\", \"-I\", \"cahn-hilliard\", \"-x\", \"c++\", \"-\", \"-o\", f\"/proc/self/fd/{fd}\", *extra], input=source, text=True, capture_output=True, pass_fds=(fd,))
    if result.returncode:
        os.close(fd)
        raise RuntimeError(result.stderr)
    fds[name] = fd
    print(json.dumps({\"compiled\": name, \"warnings\": result.stderr}), flush=True)
def run(name, args, threads=1):
    env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads), OMP_PROC_BIND=\"close\", OMP_PLACES=\"cores\", OMP_DYNAMIC=\"FALSE\")
    fd = fds[name]
    result = subprocess.run([f\"/proc/self/fd/{fd}\", *map(str,args)], text=True, capture_output=True, pass_fds=(fd,), env=env)
    return {\"returncode\":result.returncode, \"stdout\":result.stdout, \"stderr\":result.stderr}

            print(json.dumps(run(cmd.get(\"name\",\"parallel\"),cmd[\"args\"],cmd.get(\"threads\",1))),flush=True)

            break

+            const double* zm = z > 0 ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = z + 1 < nz ? row + plane : row;
+            double* out = mu + base;
+            const auto writeCell = [&](size_t x, size_t xn, size_t xp) {
+                const double cv = row[x];
+                out[x] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
                          + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(row, ym, yp, zm, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2);
+            };
+            writeCell(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                writeCell(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+            }
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                writeCell(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
             }
         }
     }
 }

codex
The first version matches the original result hashes across boundary cases, odd and even timestep counts, and 1–8 threads. On a 128³ grid with 60 steps, median compute time fell from 826 ms to 21 ms at 32 threads. I’ll check larger grids and high thread counts next for memory-placement and synchronization costs.

+            double* out = cnew + base;
+            const auto writeCell = [&](size_t x, size_t xn, size_t xp) {
+                out[x] = old[x] + dt * D *
+                         computeLaplacian(row, ym, yp, zm, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2);
+            };
+            writeCell(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                writeCell(x, x - 1, x + 1);
             }
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                writeCell(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+            }
         }
     }

+// Row pointers keep the stencil accesses contiguous and hoist Y/Z boundary
+// calculations out of the SIMD loop. X boundaries are handled separately.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* ym, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zm, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
+    const double center = row[x];
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * center) / dx2;
+    const double cyy = (yp[x] + ym[x] - 2.0 * center) / dy2;
+    const double czz = (zp[x] + zm[x] - 2.0 * center) / dz2;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;

 succeeded in 0ms:
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(cahn_hilliard_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)

add_executable(cahn_hilliard cahn_hilliard.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(cahn_hilliard PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(cahn_hilliard PRIVATE OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)

    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Every element is written before it is read. Avoid serial zero-filling
    // so the worker that first writes a page establishes its NUMA placement.
    auto cold = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    auto cnew = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    auto mu = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold.get(), nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        // Each thread swaps its own pointers, avoiding a shared buffer swap
        // and an extra barrier at the end of every time step.
        double* old = cold.get();
        double* next = cnew.get();
        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
            computeChemicalPotential(old, mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                                     gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
            cahnHilliardUpdate(next, old, mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
            std::swap(old, next);
        }
    }
    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) {
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

    mu.reset();
    cnew.reset();
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        const std::vector<double> result(cold.get(), cold.get() + gridSize);
        print_results(result, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold.get(), gridSize);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

    12	// Row pointers keep the stencil accesses contiguous and hoist Y/Z boundary
    13	// calculations out of the SIMD loop. X boundaries are handled separately.
    14	inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* ym, const double* yp,
    15	                              const double* zm, const double* zp,
    16	                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
    17	                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
    18	    const double center = row[x];
    19	    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * center) / dx2;
    20	    const double cyy = (yp[x] + ym[x] - 2.0 * center) / dy2;
    21	    const double czz = (zp[x] + zm[x] - 2.0 * center) / dz2;
    22	    return cxx + cyy + czz;
    23	}

    33	    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)

    36	            if (nx == 0) continue;
    37	            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;

    42	            const double* zp = z + 1 < nz ? row + plane : row;

    62	// Cahn-Hilliard update step
    63	void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu,
    64	                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
    65	                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    66	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    67	    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;
    68	    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    69	    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
    70	        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
    71	            if (nx == 0) continue;
    72	            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
    73	            const double* row = mu + base;
    74	            const double* ym = y > 0 ? row - nx : row;
    75	            const double* yp = y + 1 < ny ? row + nx : row;
    76	            const double* zm = z > 0 ? row - plane : row;
    77	            const double* zp = z + 1 < nz ? row + plane : row;
    78	            const double* old = cold + base;
    79	            double* out = cnew + base;
    80	            const auto writeCell = [&](size_t x, size_t xn, size_t xp) {
    81	                out[x] = old[x] + dt * D *
    82	                         computeLaplacian(row, ym, yp, zm, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2);
    83	            };

    91	            }
    92	        }
    93	    }
    94	}

    96	// Initialize concentration field
    97	void initializeConcentration(double* c, double* cnew, double* mu,
    98	                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    99	    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
   100	    
   101	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   102	    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < vol; ++idx) {
   103	        // Generate the same deterministic pseudo-random value in [-1, 1].
   104	        const double pseudo = ((((idx + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
   105	        c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   106	        // Fault in scratch pages on the same workers before timing starts.
   107	        cnew[idx] = 0.0;
   108	        mu[idx] = 0.0;
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	bool validateResult(const double* c, const size_t size) {
   113	    // Check for NaN or Inf
   114	    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   115	        const double val = c[i];
   116	        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
   117	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   118	            return false;
   119	        }
   120	    }
   121	    
   122	    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
   123	    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
   124	    double minVal = c[0];
   125	    double maxVal = c[0];
   126	    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   127	        const double val = c[i];
   128	        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
   129	        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
   130	    }
   131	    
   132	    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
   133	    
   134	    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
   135	    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
   136	        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   137	        return false;
   138	    }
   139	    
   140	    return true;
   141	}
   142	
   143	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   144	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   145	    printf("Options:\n");
   146	    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
   147	    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   148	    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   149	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
   150	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   151	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   152	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   153	}
   154	
   155	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   156	    size_t nx = 64;
   157	    size_t ny = 0;
   158	    size_t nz = 0;
   159	    int iterations = 20;
   160	    bool validate = false;
   200	    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   201	    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   202	    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
   203	    const double gamma = 0.5;
   204	    const double D = 1.0;
   205	    
   206	    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
   207	    
   208	    // Every element is written before it is read. Avoid serial zero-filling
   209	    // so the worker that first writes a page establishes its NUMA placement.
   210	    auto cold = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   211	    auto cnew = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   212	    auto mu = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   213	    
   214	    // Initialize concentration field
   215	    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   216	    initializeConcentration(cold.get(), cnew.get(), mu.get(), nx, ny, nz);
   217	    
   218	    // Run simulation
   219	    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   220	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   221	    
   222	    #pragma omp parallel
   223	    {
   224	        // Each thread swaps its own pointers, avoiding a shared buffer swap
   225	        // and an extra barrier at the end of every time step.
   226	        double* old = cold.get();
   227	        double* next = cnew.get();
   228	        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   229	            computeChemicalPotential(old, mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
   230	                                     gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
   231	            cahnHilliardUpdate(next, old, mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   232	            std::swap(old, next);
   233	        }
   234	    }
   235	    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) {
   236	        std::swap(cold, cnew);
   237	    }
   238	    
   239	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   240	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   241	    
   242	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   243	    
   244	    // Calculate performance
   245	    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
   246	    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
   247	    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   248	
   249	    mu.reset();
   250	    cnew.reset();
   251	    
   252	    // Print results for external validation
   253	    if (printResults) {
   254	        const std::vector<double> result(cold.get(), cold.get() + gridSize);
   255	        print_results(result, "Concentration");
   256	    }
   257	    
   258	    // Validation
   259	    if (validate) {
   260	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   261	        bool valid = validateResult(cold.get(), gridSize);
   262	        
   263	        if (valid) {
   264	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   265	            return 0;
   266	        } else {
   267	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   268	            return 1;
   269	        }
   270	    }
   271	    
   272	    return 0;
   273	}

+// calculations out of the SIMD loop. X boundaries are handled separately.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* ym, const double* yp,

+// calculations out of the SIMD loop. X boundaries are handled separately.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* ym, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zm, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
